- Lock the user-flagged cues together before texture polish: narrow toothed non-beak snout, folded feathered forelimbs held close to the ribs, dense body plumage, long stiff tail, and attached raised second-toe sickle claws.

Findings:

- Demoted `assets/dinosaurs/herrerasaurus-ischigualastensis-closedjaw-headblend-v1.png` from first candidate to `anatomy review` comparison because its coarse body gate remains useful but the new imagegen candidate has a stronger narrow-head and compact-forelimb read.
- Added `assets/dinosaurs/herrerasaurus-strict-crops-v1.png`, `assets/dinosaurs/herrerasaurus-review-options-v6.png`, and `tools/comfyui/scripts/make_herrerasaurus_strict_imagegen_sheet.py` for repeatable head, forelimb, hind-leg, tail, and previous-primary review.

Tested:

- Built-in image generation candidate using a strict scientific-educational prompt: full-body side-profile `Herrerasaurus ischigualastensis`, Late Triassic Ischigualasto dry floodplain, lightly built early saurischian predator, horizontal body, long single balancing tail, narrow elongated carnivorous skull, calm closed mouth, exactly two hind legs, visible three-toed theropod feet, and two non-weight-bearing grasping forelimbs under the chest. The prompt explicitly rejected extra legs, six-legged silhouettes, duplicate arms, duplicate tails, forelimbs touching the ground, huge dangling human-like fingers, tiny T. rex arms, bulky Tyrannosaurus head, Plateosaurus herbivore drift, bird beaks, feathers, wings, hidden feet, text, labels, logos, and watermarks.

Findings:

- The selected compact-forelimb candidate is the clearest Herrerasaurus app image so far. It improves the calmer narrow head, dry-ground body gate, full tail, and small but visible forelimb read compared with the previous head-blend primary.
- Keep it as `count-level pass`, not final approval. Exact finger count, hand proportions, and toe anatomy still need reference review. A future early-saurischian LoRA or i2i route should reproduce this full-body read while tightening hands and feet.

### Herrerasaurus compact-hand imagegen v2 pass

Goal:

- Improve the Herrerasaurus first image without repeating the old failures: tiny T. rex arms, wide roaring mouth, long dangling human-like fingers, or body drift toward a bulky large theropod.
- Update the hand target from a misleading simple three-finger rule to a Herrerasaurus-specific gate: three main clawed digits may dominate, while any fourth/fifth digits should read as tiny vestigial outer digits rather than equally long fingers.

Changed:

- Added `assets/dinosaurs/herrerasaurus-ischigualastensis-compacthands-imagegen-v2.png` as the first `count-level pass` Herrerasaurus candidate.
- Added `assets/dinosaurs/herrerasaurus-ischigualastensis-balancedhands-imagegen-v2.png` as an `anatomy review` comparison because it has a polished body and arm length but a higher hand-count risk.
- Demoted `assets/dinosaurs/herrerasaurus-ischigualastensis-strict-imagegen-alt-v1.png` from first candidate to `anatomy review` comparison because the new v2 scene improves atlas-scale body polish and compact folded-hand readability.
- Added `assets/dinosaurs/herrerasaurus-compacthands-crops-v2.png`, `assets/dinosaurs/herrerasaurus-review-options-v7.png`, and `tools/comfyui/scripts/make_herrerasaurus_compacthands_imagegen_sheet.py` for repeatable closed-head, compact-hand, hind-leg/feet, tail, balanced-v2 hand-risk, previous-primary, and long-arm comparison review.

Tested:

- Built-in image generation candidate using a strict scientific-educational prompt: full-body side-profile `Herrerasaurus ischigualastensis`, Late Triassic Ischigualasto dry floodplain, lightly built early saurischian predator, slim horizontal torso, narrow elongated closed-mouth skull, short-to-medium neck, long single straight balancing tail, exactly two hind legs only, visible three-toed theropod feet, and exactly two slim forelimbs folded under the chest. The prompt explicitly rejected extra limbs, six-legged read, duplicate forearms, hidden hind leg, fused feet, giant dangling claws, human-like hands, four or five obvious fingers, tiny T. rex arms, open mouth, oversized Tyrannosaurus skull, bird beak, feathers, wing arms, Plateosaurus herbivore face, quadruped stance, cropped anatomy, text, labels, logos, and watermarks.

Findings:

- The selected compact-hand v2 candidate is the strongest Herrerasaurus app-scale image so far: full dry side profile, closed narrow head, single tail, two visible hind legs, and smaller non-weight-bearing hands.
- Keep it as `count-level pass`, not final approval. The hand crop still needs a reference check for the Herrerasaurus-specific three-main-clawed-digit plus tiny vestigial outer-digit target, and exact toe anatomy remains pending.

### Herrerasaurus compact-hand body-lock control route

Goal:

- Keep the current v2 natural image first while adding a stronger project-owned structure route for the next ControlNet/i2i pass.
- Preserve the Herrerasaurus-specific early-saurischian body gate: closed narrow head, slim horizontal torso, full single tail, exactly two hind legs, dry three-toed feet, and compact folded forelimbs that are longer than T. rex arms but not long dangling hooks.

Changed:

- Added `assets/dinosaurs/herrerasaurus-ischigualastensis-bodylock-guide-v1.png` as a `structure reference`, not final paleoart.
- Added `assets/dinosaurs/herrerasaurus-bodylock-crops-v3.png` for guide-versus-current review of head, compact hands, body/tail, hind legs/feet, balanced-hand risk, and long-arm risk.
- Added `assets/dinosaurs/herrerasaurus-review-options-v8.png` to compare the current v2 image, the new body-lock guide, balanced-arm hand-count risk, previous strict compact v1, strict dangling-hand risk, closed-jaw body gate, and long-arm IP-Control comparison.
- Added `tools/comfyui/scripts/draw_herrerasaurus_bodylock_guide.py` and `tools/comfyui/lora_training/early_saurischian_herrerasaurus/bodylock_prompt_schedule.json` for repeatable guide-conditioned experiments.

Findings:

- The current v2 candidate remains the best natural app candidate. The new guide is useful because it separates the exact next-route target from the current natural render: compact folded forelimbs, three main clawed digits plus tiny outer vestigial digits, two hind legs, dry feet, closed narrow head, and full tail.
- Reject future Herrerasaurus outputs if they are prettier but shrink the arms into T. rex proportions, create long dangling hand claws, make four or five equal fingers dominate, hide feet/tail, reopen the mouth into a monster gape, or bulk the body toward Allosaurus/T. rex.

### Ankylosaurus wide-armor imagegen v3 pass

Goal:

- Improve the current Ankylosaurus first image without losing the successful one-tail, four-leg, and single attached tail-club gates.
- Prioritize a broader blunt armored skull, very short neck, low heavy shoulders, rounded armor rows, planted feet, and a compact fused oval club.

Changed:

- Added `assets/dinosaurs/ankylosaurus-magniventris-widearmor-imagegen-v3.png` as the first `count-level pass` Ankylosaurus candidate.
- Added `assets/dinosaurs/ankylosaurus-magniventris-widearmor-comparison-imagegen-v3.png` as an `anatomy review` comparison because it keeps the low-body silhouette but reads longer and less compact at app scale.
- Demoted `assets/dinosaurs/ankylosaurus-magniventris-lowcompact-imagegen-v2.png` from first candidate to `anatomy review` comparison because v3 improves the broad shoulder, skull, and armor identity read.
- Added `assets/dinosaurs/ankylosaurus-widearmor-crops-v3.png`, `assets/dinosaurs/ankylosaurus-review-options-v8.png`, and `tools/comfyui/scripts/make_ankylosaurus_widearmor_imagegen_sheet.py` for repeatable full-body, head, armor-row, tail-club, foot, previous-primary, and older club-risk review.

Tested:

- Built-in image generation candidate using a strict scientific-educational prompt: full-body side-view `Ankylosaurus magniventris`, Late Cretaceous dry floodplain woodland edge, very low broad squat armored quadruped, compact wide wedge-shaped skull, very short neck, short blunt snout, heavy shoulders, dense rounded osteoderms and oval bony armor plates, four short sturdy legs with all feet separated on the ground, thick low tail, and exactly one compact single fused oval bony tail club directly attached to the tail tip. The prompt explicitly rejected crocodile heads, long narrow snouts, turtle-shell domes, pangolin scales, Stegosaurus plates, tall dorsal spikes, side fantasy spikes, mace spikes, two balls on the tail, missing tail clubs, hidden feet, hooves, extra limbs, biped posture, text, labels, and watermarks.

Findings:

- The selected v3 candidate is the strongest current familiar Ankylosaurus read: it keeps four planted feet, one tail, one attached compact club, low rounded armor rows, and a broader blunt head/shoulder profile than the previous low-compact v2.
- Keep it as `count-level pass`, not final approval. Exact skull proportions, toe details, and armor-row layout still need reference review or a future Ankylosauridae LoRA/i2i refinement.

## App Review Workflow Notes

- The review page now supports browser-local candidate decisions: `적합`, `보류`, and `탈락`.
- Decisions are stored in `localStorage` under `dino-atlas-candidate-review-decisions-v1`, separately from the manual representative selection. This keeps quick human QA notes available while avoiding accidental source promotion.
- Marking a manually selected representative as `탈락` clears that manual representative choice, so a rejected candidate does not continue to lead the atlas gallery.

- Generation route dashboard v1 is available at `assets/dinosaurs/generation-route-dashboard-v1.png`, with its source summary in `tools/comfyui/outputs/generation-route-summary.json`. Use it as the compact handoff sheet for each taxon's current primary image, primary structure guide, next LoRA/i2i route, and hard rejection rule before starting another ComfyUI batch.
- The review page now mirrors the same next-route data per selected taxon: current candidate review, identity gate, primary control guide thumbnail, LoRA/i2i route summary, and hard rejection rule appear in one flow, with a route-copy action for the next ComfyUI batch note.
- `tools/comfyui/next-generation-batch-plan.md` is the prioritized run queue for the next sample loop. It starts with Velociraptor, Stegosaurus, Triceratops, Ankylosaurus, and Plateosaurus because those still carry the highest identity or limb/plate/tail-club risk, then lists ControlNet, schedule-prompt, and low-denoise i2i probe commands for each taxon.
- First Velociraptor next-route smoke run completed from `dromaeosaur_feathered/identity_bodylock_prompt_schedule.json` with seed `2026070221`. The generated output is preserved as `assets/dinosaurs/velociraptor-mongoliensis-smoke-schedule-rejected-v20.png` and stays `diagnostic only`: it proves the schedule path works, but the animal loses dense feathered dromaeosaur identity and does not preserve a reliable attached raised second-toe sickle claw. The next useful attempt should not rely on prompt schedule alone; use the identity body-lock ControlNet/i2i route or stronger dromaeosaur LoRA.
- Velociraptor clean-guide i2i v21 tested `assets/dinosaurs/velociraptor-mongoliensis-identity-bodylock-guide-clean-v1.png` at denoise `0.56` and `0.68`. Removing labels solved text bleed, but the outputs still remain `diagnostic only` because they lose dense feathered dromaeosaur identity and reliable attached sickle-toe evidence. Future Velociraptor i2i/ControlNet should use the clean guide as the source, but needs stronger dromaeosaur feather/foot conditioning before any promotion.
- Added `tools/comfyui/scripts/run_schedule_i2i_experiment.py` to combine taxon-specific prompt schedules with i2i source images. Velociraptor v22 tested the clean guide with `identity_bodylock_folded_arm_01` and the current v9 candidate with `identity_bodylock_head_foot_01`; both remain `diagnostic only`. The new runner is useful, but Velociraptor still needs stronger dromaeosaur LoRA or foot-specific control to beat v9.
- Velociraptor identity-control v23 tested `assets/dinosaurs/velociraptor-mongoliensis-identity-bodylock-guide-clean-v1.png` as ControlNet with seed `2026070201`, strength `0.50`, and end percent `0.64`; v24 tested the current `assets/dinosaurs/velociraptor-mongoliensis-small-sickle-imagegen-v9.png` as IP-Adapter reference with the clean identity guide, seed `2026070261`, weight `0.38`, and denoise `0.42`. Both remain `diagnostic only`: v23 weakens feathered dromaeosaur identity and attached sickle-toe evidence, while v24 collapses into flat guide-like art. The results are preserved as `assets/dinosaurs/velociraptor-mongoliensis-identity-controlnet-rejected-v23.png`, `assets/dinosaurs/velociraptor-mongoliensis-v9-identity-ipi2i-rejected-v24.png`, `assets/dinosaurs/velociraptor-identity-v23-v24-rejection-sheet.png`, `assets/dinosaurs/velociraptor-identity-v23-v24-rejection-crops.png`, and `tools/comfyui/lora_training/dromaeosaur_feathered/review/velociraptor_identity_v23_v24_review.json`. The next real quality step is a custom `dromaeosaur_feathered` LoRA, stronger line/depth foot control, reviewed dromaeosaur foot structure reference, or reference-conditioned i2i route that preserves the non-beak toothed snout, visible folded hands, long stiff tail, feathered body, grounded feet, and folded forelimbs while making the raised sickle claws anatomically anchored to the second toes. Do not promote plain foot-only i2i outputs; the v10 probe produced floating/detached claws or oversized hook-like talons. Do not promote plain whole-body prompt-only v11 outputs; they reduced some claw risk by losing feathered dromaeosaur identity and drifting into generic theropod or bird-like legs. Do not promote whole-body guide-source ControlNet/IP-i2i outputs like v23/v24 when they lose plumage, sickle-toe evidence, or app-ready natural paleoart quality. Do not promote simple hook-reduction masks like v26/v28 when they either lengthen the walking toes, weaken the dromaeosaur foot read, or change too little to solve attached second-toe topology. Do not promote head-only low-denoise masks like v27/v29 when they reduce bird-eye drift only subtly or by softening dromaeosaur head plumage, making the snout more generic, or failing to improve skull/eye proportions enough to beat v9. Do not promote local toe-only masks like v30 unless close crops prove a cleaner attached second-toe claw while preserving grounded walking toes. Do not promote the current SD1.5 Velociraptor LoRA+i2i route; it remains too flat and bird-like.

The next useful step is a real Stegosauridae LoRA or a stronger reviewed plate-structure training set, not another pasted plate-graft naturalization, prompt-only plate-count sweep, generic/practical-fx LoRA plate-band i2i, or v6-reference IP-Control sweep with overpainted plates alone. Preserve the clearer v6 alternating row cue, natural low body, thick broad separate bony plates, visible plate gaps, and countable four-spike thagomizer while locking exact two-row placement, plate-surface texture, and tail-spike count. Do not promote outputs that improve texture but collapse into a single connected sail, petal-like plates, identical roof teeth, pasted flat panels, translucent plate boundaries, comb-like spines, shoulder fans, neck-frill plate clusters, visible frame/corner artifacts, text/label artifacts, generic herbivore body drift, or a missing/non-four-spike thagomizer. Whole-body low-denoise i2i like v48, narrow seam edits like v49/v51, micro seam edits like v58, plate-base/gap edits like v59/v60, broad plate-graft attempts like v61/v62, prompt-only synthetic seeds like v63, weak generic/practical-fx LoRA plate-band edits like v56/v57/v64, and plate-silhouette IP-Control like v65 can be kept as comparison evidence, but they are not enough for primary promotion unless the plate and tail gates visibly improve together. Broad full-row masks like v50 should be avoided unless paired with stronger structure conditioning, because they soften the body and plate texture. Plain canny ControlNet from either the labeled topology guide or the clean guide should not be promoted by itself: v52 bleeds labels and v53 still turns the plates into comb/sail/spike-row reads. V6+clean-guide IP-Control and v6+photo-silhouette IP-Control should also stay diagnostic unless a future implementation can keep Stegosaurus identity, separated plates, and four tail spikes together; v54 loses Stegosaurus identity, v55 fuses plates into a fan/sail, and v65 drifts into generic herbivore bodies with comb/fan/neck-frill plates. Weak generic Dinosaur_Generator_v2 LoRA and dinosaur_practical_fx LoRA over the dorsal plate band should also stay diagnostic; v56 is mostly a near-copy, v57 softens texture without solving two-row topology, and v64 preserves the body but still does not create a stronger plate read. Very low-denoise v58, base/gap v59/v60, graft v61/v62, prompt-only v63, practical-fx v64, and silhouette-control v65 are diagnostic because they either preserve anatomy while changing too little or improve plate count/texture by breaking natural integration, body identity, or thagomizer reliability.
